<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-48555993" data-id="48555993" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Privacy|Hardware" data-score="340" data-comments="177" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555993" data-time="1781620617">
<a class="thumb" href="https://vickiboykis.com/2026/06/15/running-local-models-is-good-now/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://vickiboykis.com/images/refactor_gemma.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='vickiboykis.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://vickiboykis.com/2026/06/15/running-local-models-is-good-now/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running local models is good now</span><span class='zh'>本地运行模型现在已经可行</span></a><span class="dom">vickiboykis.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recent advances in open-source models, tooling, and consumer hardware have made running local models practical again.</span><span class='zh'>近来开源模型、工具链和硬件水平的提升让本地运行模型重新成为现实，带来隐私、更低延迟和离线可用性等优势，同时在性能与可更新性方面仍存在权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recent advances in open-source models, tooling, and consumer hardware have made running local models practical again. Local inference offers privacy, lower latency, and offline reliability, though trade-offs in performance and updatability remain.</span><span class='zh'>近来开源模型、工具链和硬件水平的提升让本地运行模型重新成为现实，带来隐私、更低延迟和离线可用性等优势，同时在性能与可更新性方面仍存在权衡。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://ciechanow.ski/mechanical-watch/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ciechanow.ski/images/og/watch.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='ciechanow.ski' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ciechanow.ski/mechanical-watch/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mechanical Watch (2022)</span><span class='zh'>机械表（2022）</span></a><span class="dom">ciechanow.ski</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mechanical Watch (2022) breaks down the fundamentals: a mainspring stores energy, a gear train transmits it, and the escapement and balance wheel regulate time.</span><span class='zh'>《机械手表（2022）》分解原理：发条储存能量，通过齿轮传动，靠擒纵机构和摆轮来调控走时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mechanical Watch (2022) breaks down the fundamentals: a mainspring stores energy, a gear train transmits it, and the escapement and balance wheel regulate time. It also discusses sources of error—friction, temperature, and wear—and why accuracy remains a balancing act between durability and precision.</span><span class='zh'>《机械手表（2022）》分解原理：发条储存能量，通过齿轮传动，靠擒纵机构和摆轮来调控走时。文中还讨论了摩擦、温度和磨损等误差来源，以及在耐用性与精准度之间的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5google.com/2026/06/15/google-chromes-next-update-will-mark-the-end-of-popular-ad-blockers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Chrome update will close the door on ad blockers</span><span class='zh'>谷歌 Chrome 更新将关闭对广告拦截插件的支持</span></a><span class="dom">9to5google.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chrome&#x27;s upcoming update tightens extension APIs, effectively phasing out most ad blockers by restricting how they can intercept or modify page content.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome的最新更新将收紧扩展API，通过限制插件拦截或修改网页内容的能力来削弱大多数广告拦截工具的效果，用户体验可能受影响，开发者需适应新约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chrome&#x27;s upcoming update tightens extension APIs, effectively phasing out most ad blockers by restricting how they can intercept or modify page content. The move could degrade ad-blocking effectiveness for users while developers adapt to new constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome的最新更新将收紧扩展API，通过限制插件拦截或修改网页内容的能力来削弱大多数广告拦截工具的效果，用户体验可能受影响，开发者需适应新约束。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/06/15/feds-freaked-over-fable-5-after-simple-fix-this-code-prompt-not-jailbreak-says-researcher/5255827" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Feds freaked over Fable 5 after &#x27;fix this code&#x27;, not jailbreak, say researchers</span><span class='zh'>研究人员称，对Fable 5的担忧并非因越狱，而是源于修这段代码的提示</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers say the Fable 5 incident wasn’t about jailbreaks but a simple fix-this-code prompt that triggered automated defenses.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员指出，对Fable 5的担忧并非源自越狱风险，而是一个简单的修这段代码提示触发的自动防护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers say the Fable 5 incident wasn’t about jailbreaks but a simple fix-this-code prompt that triggered automated defenses. The episode highlights how prompt patterns can provoke legal or policy alarms even when the underlying tech isn&#x27;t compromised.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员指出，对Fable 5的担忧并非源自越狱风险，而是一个简单的修这段代码提示触发的自动防护。此事揭示了提示模式如何诱发策略阻断与调查的风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A backdoor in a LinkedIn job offer</span><span class='zh'>LinkedIn招聘广告中的后门</span></a><span class="dom">roman.pt</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A backdoor was discovered embedded in a LinkedIn job offer, illustrating how the recruiting process can be weaponized to compromise targets.</span><span class='zh'>在 LinkedIn 的一个招聘信息中发现了后门，暴露了招聘流程如何被用来对目标进行入侵。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A backdoor was discovered embedded in a LinkedIn job offer, illustrating how the recruiting process can be weaponized to compromise targets. The post explains how attackers used the job posting to deliver malware or harvest credentials, highlighting the risk of social engineering on professional networks. It underscores the need for stricter validation of job postings and better phishing defenses by platforms and users.</span><span class='zh'>在 LinkedIn 的一个招聘信息中发现了后门，暴露了招聘流程如何被用来对目标进行入侵。攻击者可能通过该职位发布来投放恶意软件或窃取凭据，凸显了职业社交网络中的钓鱼风险。强调平台和用户都需加强对招聘信息的验证与安全防护。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1479</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546294' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>278</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48550693" data-id="48550693" data-tags="Programming" data-score="437" data-comments="138" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550693" data-time="1781585196">
<a class="thumb" href="https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260615-00/?p=112419" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=devblogs.microsoft.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>devblogs.microsoft.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260615-00/?p=112419" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The time the x86 emulator team found code so bad they fixed it during emulation</span><span class='zh'>X86 模拟器团队在仿真中直接修复糟糕代码的故事</span></a><span class="dom">devblogs.microsoft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>While running legacy x86 code, the emulator team uncovered a section so badly written that it risked correctness.</span><span class='zh'>在仿真环境中运行遗留的 x86 代码时，团队发现了一段异常糟糕的实现，严重影响正确性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>While running legacy x86 code, the emulator team uncovered a section so badly written that it risked correctness. They patched the behavior directly in the emulator during the debugging session, effectively fixing the issue without modifying the source. The story highlights how emulation can surface design flaws and enable quick, in-situ fixes for stubborn code.</span><span class='zh'>在仿真环境中运行遗留的 x86 代码时，团队发现了一段异常糟糕的实现，严重影响正确性。他们在仿真过程中直接对行为进行了修正，几乎是在现场完成了修复，而无需改动源代码。这个故事展示了仿真工具在暴露设计缺陷并实现快速就地修复方面的强大力量。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>437</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550693' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>138</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48552844" data-id="48552844" data-tags="Gaming|Design" data-score="199" data-comments="62" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552844" data-time="1781603209">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tck.mn/blog/correlated-randomness-sts2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Correlated randomness in Slay the Spire 2</span><span class='zh'>Slay the Spire 2中的相关随机性设计</span></a><span class="dom">tck.mn</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Slay the Spire 2 adopts correlated randomness to balance player agency and fairness, ensuring strategic decisions matter while avoiding extreme luck twists.</span><span class='zh'>在Slay the Spire 2中实现相关随机性设计，以在玩法多样性和公平性之间取得平衡，确保玩家策略决策真正影响结果，同时避免运气极端波动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Slay the Spire 2 adopts correlated randomness to balance player agency and fairness, ensuring strategic decisions matter while avoiding extreme luck twists. The design concept helps maintain challenge and variety as the game evolves.</span><span class='zh'>在Slay the Spire 2中实现相关随机性设计，以在玩法多样性和公平性之间取得平衡，确保玩家策略决策真正影响结果，同时避免运气极端波动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: Has anyone replaced Claude/GPT with a local model for daily coding?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：有没有人用本地模型替代 Claude/GPT 进行日常编码？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Users discuss replacing cloud-based copilots with locally hosted models for everyday coding tasks.</span><span class='zh'>社区在讨论是否可用本地部署的模型来替代云端的助理进行日常编码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Users discuss replacing cloud-based copilots with locally hosted models for everyday coding tasks. Local models can improve privacy and reduce latency, but may require beefier hardware and careful tuning to approach Claude/GPT capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>社区在讨论是否可用本地部署的模型来替代云端的助理进行日常编码。虽然本地模型提升隐私与降低延迟的潜力很大，但往往需要更强大的硬件与精心调优以接近 Claude/GPT 的能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tinywind.io" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TinyWind: A pixel pirate sailing game with real wind physics (380k+ kms sailed)</span><span class='zh'>TinyWind：一款具备真实风力物理的像素海盗航海游戏（航行里程超过38万公里）</span></a><span class="dom">tinywind.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TinyWind delivers a pixel-art sailing experience driven by plausible wind physics, giving a tactile sense of sailing.</span><span class='zh'>这款像素风帆船游戏以贴近真实的风力物理为核心，带来更具手感的航海体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TinyWind delivers a pixel-art sailing experience driven by plausible wind physics, giving a tactile sense of sailing. With over 380,000 kilometers sailed by players, it demonstrates strong engagement with physics-based gameplay and sparks ideas for indie developers to model wind without heavy graphics.</span><span class='zh'>这款像素风帆船游戏以贴近真实的风力物理为核心，带来更具手感的航海体验。玩家累计航行里程已超38万公里，体现了对基于物理的玩法的强烈投入，并为独立开发者在无需繁重美术资源的前提下实现可信风场提供了思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.richardosgood.com/posts/banned-book-library/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Banned book library in a wi-fi smart light bulb</span><span class='zh'>Wi‑Fi 智能灯泡中的被禁书库</span></a><span class="dom">richardosgood.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Wi‑Fi smart light bulb secretly hosts a library of banned or hard-to-find texts, turning a household device into a portable knowledge archive.</span><span class='zh'>一颗具备 Wi‑Fi 的智能灯泡成了一个迷你禁书库，将文本打包进常见的物联网设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Wi‑Fi smart light bulb secretly hosts a library of banned or hard-to-find texts, turning a household device into a portable knowledge archive. The project provokes thoughts about censorship, distributed aesthetics, and the potential of IoT as a stealth repository.</span><span class='zh'>一颗具备 Wi‑Fi 的智能灯泡成了一个迷你禁书库，将文本打包进常见的物联网设备。该项目展示了硬件与本地网络如何成为信息存储与传播的新载体，也引发对数字审查与数据持久性的思考。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://bobdahacker.com/blog/fifa-hack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://bobdahacker.com/static/images/blogs/fifa/blogHeader.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='bobdahacker.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bobdahacker.com/blog/fifa-hack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I Could&#x27;ve Rickrolled the FIFA World Cup. All I Needed Was My ID</span><span class='zh'>我几乎可以把 FIFA 世界杯变成 Rickroll 的。只需要我的 ID</span></a><span class="dom">bobdahacker.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An ID-based vulnerability around a major sporting site allowed simple manipulation of content to surface a Rickroll, illustrating how predictable identifiers can enable unexpected redirects or access.</span><span class='zh'>仅凭一个 ID 就能在 FIFA 世界杯相关页面触发跳转到 Rickroll，暴露了基于 ID 的访问控制薄弱和可预测行为的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An ID-based vulnerability around a major sporting site allowed simple manipulation of content to surface a Rickroll, illustrating how predictable identifiers can enable unexpected redirects or access. The post underscores the risks of URL-level identifiers and highlights the need for robust authentication and access controls in large public-facing apps.</span><span class='zh'>仅凭一个 ID 就能在 FIFA 世界杯相关页面触发跳转到 Rickroll，暴露了基于 ID 的访问控制薄弱和可预测行为的风险。这说明在面向公众的应用中，需加强认证与访问控制，避免通过简单标识符被滥用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece questions what happened to the traditional nerd archetype as tech culture shifts and becomes mainstream.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章在质疑随着科技文化的转变，传统书呆子形象到底去了哪里。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece questions what happened to the traditional nerd archetype as tech culture shifts and becomes mainstream.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章在质疑随着科技文化的转变，传统书呆子形象到底去了哪里。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/2026/06/15/curl-summer-of-bliss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Curl will not accept vulnerability reports during July 2026</span><span class='zh'>Curl 将在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告</span></a><span class="dom">daniel.haxx.se</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The curl project announces a July 2026 hiatus from vulnerability submissions to coordinate disclosures and focus on fixes, with researchers advised to submit reports before or after the window.</span><span class='zh'>Curl 项目宣布在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告，以便协调披露时间并集中处理修复工作，建议研究人员在此窗口前后提交报告。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The curl project announces a July 2026 hiatus from vulnerability submissions to coordinate disclosures and focus on fixes, with researchers advised to submit reports before or after the window. The move highlights the realities of coordinating security work in a large, open-source project and sets expectations for researchers.</span><span class='zh'>Curl 项目宣布在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告，以便协调披露时间并集中处理修复工作，建议研究人员在此窗口前后提交报告。此举凸显了在大型开源项目中协同安全工作的现实挑战，并为研究人员设定预期。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://michaelenger.com/blog/i-love-the-computer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I Love the Computer</span><span class='zh'>我爱电脑</span></a><span class="dom">michaelenger.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A personal meditation celebrating the beauty and power of computers, weaving hardware, software, and tinkering into a narrative about craft and curiosity.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇个人随笔，赞美电脑的美与力量，讲述硬件、软件和动手改造如何汇聚成工艺与好奇心的故事。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A personal meditation celebrating the beauty and power of computers, weaving hardware, software, and tinkering into a narrative about craft and curiosity. The piece resonates with engineers and hackers who still find joy in solving problems at the metal and code level, not just apps. It serves as a reminder that computing remains a hands-on pursuit with tangible feedback.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇个人随笔，赞美电脑的美与力量，讲述硬件、软件和动手改造如何汇聚成工艺与好奇心的故事。对于仍然沉浸于硬件与代码之解题乐趣的工程师与黑客，这篇文字有共鸣。它提醒人们，计算仍是一门需要动手实践、能获得直接反馈的领域。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">21</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gmalandrakis.com/writings/ad-economicum.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Peopleless economy? Not technically impossible</span><span class='zh'>无人经济？并非技术上不可能</span></a><span class="dom">gmalandrakis.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An essay argues that a &#x27;peopleless&#x27; economy powered by automation and AI isn&#x27;t technically impossible, though it would require radical shifts in productivity measurement, wealth distribution, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>一篇文章称由自动化和人工智能驱动的“无人经济”并非不可能，但需要在生产率衡量、财富分配和治理方面进行根本性转变。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An essay argues that a &#x27;peopleless&#x27; economy powered by automation and AI isn&#x27;t technically impossible, though it would require radical shifts in productivity measurement, wealth distribution, and governance. It surveys practical challenges—from consumer demand to ownership models—and suggests policy levers to enable high-automation prosperity without sidelining society.</span><span class='zh'>一篇文章称由自动化和人工智能驱动的“无人经济”并非不可能，但需要在生产率衡量、财富分配和治理方面进行根本性转变。文中分析了从需求端到所有权模式等实际挑战，并提出在高自动化时代实现繁荣的政策杠杆。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>267</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547062' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>499</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48549918" data-id="48549918" data-tags="AI|Business|Programming" data-score="153" data-comments="69" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48549918" data-time="1781578077">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://runtimewire.com/article/microsoft-github-aws-ai-capacity-crunch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft turns to AWS as GitHub faces AI capacity crunch</span><span class='zh'>在GitHub AI容量吃紧时，微软转向AWS</span></a><span class="dom">runtimewire.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>With GitHub&#x27;s AI capacity strained, Microsoft is turning to AWS to provision extra compute and keep Copilot and other AI features humming.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub的AI容量吃紧，微软正转向AWS以获取额外算力，确保Copilot等AI功能持续运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>With GitHub&#x27;s AI capacity strained, Microsoft is turning to AWS to provision extra compute and keep Copilot and other AI features humming. The cross-cloud approach reflects broader industry realities around capacity, redundancy, and developer productivity.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub的AI容量吃紧，微软正转向AWS以获取额外算力，确保Copilot等AI功能持续运行。跨云策略反映出行业在容量、冗余和开发者生产力方面的现实需求。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://andreklein.net/your-epub-is-fine-kobo-disagrees-blame-adobe/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://andreklein.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/gandalf-1024x625.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='andreklein.net' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://andreklein.net/your-epub-is-fine-kobo-disagrees-blame-adobe/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Your ePub Is fine</span><span class='zh'>你的 ePub 没问题</span></a><span class="dom">andreklein.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kobo pushes back against blaming Adobe for ePub issues, arguing the format remains solid and that rendering problems are often caused by apps or readers rather than the standard.</span><span class='zh'>在围绕 ePub 兼容性的争议中，Kobo 反对把问题归咎于 Adobe，认为该格式本身仍然稳健，渲染问题往往来自具体应用与设备实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kobo pushes back against blaming Adobe for ePub issues, arguing the format remains solid and that rendering problems are often caused by apps or readers rather than the standard. The post reframes the controversy as a shared interoperability challenge across devices, tools, and publishers, not a single vendor fault. This has practical implications for developers and distributors aiming for broad ePub compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>在围绕 ePub 兼容性的争议中，Kobo 反对把问题归咎于 Adobe，认为该格式本身仍然稳健，渲染问题往往来自具体应用与设备实现。该观点将难题定位为读写器、工具和出版商共同面临的互操作挑战，而非某个厂商的错。此事对开发者和出版方在跨设备兼容性上的决策具有现实意义。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>890</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48533848' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>304</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48544396" data-id="48544396" data-tags="Programming" data-score="324" data-comments="87" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544396" data-time="1781544257">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Typst 0.15.0 brings new features and fixes to this lightweight document typesetting system, enhancing typography controls and rendering performance.</span><span class='zh'>Typst 0.15.0 带来新特性和修复，提升排版控制与渲染性能，改进对齐、字体和自动化能力，方便作者更高效地排版。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Typst 0.15.0 brings new features and fixes to this lightweight document typesetting system, enhancing typography controls and rendering performance. The update tightens alignment, fonts, and automation capabilities for writers.</span><span class='zh'>Typst 0.15.0 带来新特性和修复，提升排版控制与渲染性能，改进对齐、字体和自动化能力，方便作者更高效地排版。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.economist.com/by-invitation/2026/06/15/humanity-isnt-ready-for-the-coming-intelligence-explosion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Humanity isn&#x27;t ready for the coming intelligence explosion</span><span class='zh'>人类尚未为来临的智能爆炸做好准备</span></a><span class="dom">economist.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative piece argues that society and institutions are unprepared for a rapid &#x27;intelligence explosion&#x27; driven by AI, warning of governance, workforce, and ethical blind spots.</span><span class='zh'>一篇有争议的文章警告，社会与机构尚未为快速的‘智能爆炸’做好准备，涉及治理、就业与伦理等盲点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative piece argues that society and institutions are unprepared for a rapid &#x27;intelligence explosion&#x27; driven by AI, warning of governance, workforce, and ethical blind spots. It calls for proactive policy, risk management, and adaptable institutions to keep pace with accelerating AI capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>一篇有争议的文章警告，社会与机构尚未为快速的‘智能爆炸’做好准备，涉及治理、就业与伦理等盲点。文章倡导前瞻性政策和可适应的治理框架，以应对日益增强的人工智能能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.monash.edu/news/articles/copper-drug-restores-memory-and-clears-toxic-alzheimers-proteins" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Copper transport drug restores memory and clears toxic Alzheimer&#x27;s proteins</span><span class='zh'>铜运输药物能恢复记忆并清除有毒的阿尔茨海默蛋白</span></a><span class="dom">monash.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A copper-transport drug showing memory restoration and clearance of toxic Alzheimer&#x27;s proteins in preclinical work points to a novel therapeutic angle.</span><span class='zh'>早期研究显示一种铜运输药物在前瞻性实验中能恢复记忆并清除与阿尔茨海默相关的有毒蛋白，提示金属稳态在神经退行性疾病治疗中的潜在方向，尽管仍处于初步阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A copper-transport drug showing memory restoration and clearance of toxic Alzheimer&#x27;s proteins in preclinical work points to a novel therapeutic angle. While early, the result underscores metal-homeostasis biology as a potential avenue for neurodegenerative treatment.</span><span class='zh'>早期研究显示一种铜运输药物在前瞻性实验中能恢复记忆并清除与阿尔茨海默相关的有毒蛋白，提示金属稳态在神经退行性疾病治疗中的潜在方向，尽管仍处于初步阶段。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Outreach to strangers can unlock unexpected opportunities, insights, and collaborations that aren’t visible through typical networks. The piece argues for purposeful, respectful emailing and offers practical tips for proposing value, setting boundaries, and building genuine connections.</span><span class='zh'>主动给陌生人发邮件可以开启意想不到的机会、洞见与合作，这些往往在常规网络中难以发现。文章主张有目的、尊重的邮件沟通，并提供提出价值、设定边界与建立真诚人际关系的实用要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fred.stlouisfed.org/series/IPG33591S" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US battery manufacturing output continues to break records</span><span class='zh'>美国电池制造产出持续刷新纪录</span></a><span class="dom">fred.stlouisfed.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>US battery manufacturing output is hitting new records, driven by accelerating EV production and stationary storage demand.</span><span class='zh'>美国电池制造产出达到新高，受到电动车和储能需求快速增长的推动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>US battery manufacturing output is hitting new records, driven by accelerating EV production and stationary storage demand. The surge signals a ramp-up of domestic gigafactories and supply-chain scale, with implications for jobs, prices, and national energy security. Continued capacity expansion will depend on critical materials, policy support, and industrial strategy.</span><span class='zh'>美国电池制造产出达到新高，受到电动车和储能需求快速增长的推动。产出激增反映了国内巨型工厂投资和供应链扩张的影响，涉及就业、成本和国家能源安全。未来产能的持续扩张将取决于关键材料、政策支持等因素。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://forgottenbytes.net/commander_keen.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Game Engine White Papers: Commander Keen</span><span class='zh'>游戏引擎白皮书：Commander Keen</span></a><span class="dom">forgottenbytes.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A set of white papers revisiting the Commander Keen engine, offering historical insight into early platformers&#x27; tech choices and how those decisions shaped modern game engines.</span><span class='zh'>一组白皮书回顾 Commander Keen 引擎，揭示早期平台游戏在渲染、内存管理和工具链等方面的技术选择，以及这些决定如何影响现代游戏引擎的演进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A set of white papers revisiting the Commander Keen engine, offering historical insight into early platformers&#x27; tech choices and how those decisions shaped modern game engines. The notes reveal timeless lessons on rendering, memory management, and toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>一组白皮书回顾 Commander Keen 引擎，揭示早期平台游戏在渲染、内存管理和工具链等方面的技术选择，以及这些决定如何影响现代游戏引擎的演进。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Salesforce is acquiring Fin, a customer messaging platform formerly known as Intercom, for $3.6B, a move to deepen its customer engagement stack. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hetzner raised prices for dedicated servers by 3-4x, a move that could ripple through small businesses and hosting providers relying on its hardware. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tamnd/kage" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Kage – Shadow any website to a single binary for offline viewing</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Kage — 将任意网站打包为离线可用的单一二进制文件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kage bundles a website into a single standalone binary, enabling offline viewing without a browser or network.</span><span class='zh'>Kage 将任意网站打包成一个独立的可执行文件，离线也能浏览。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kage bundles a website into a single standalone binary, enabling offline viewing without a browser or network. It makes distribution and archiving of a site trivial for demos, edge devices, or offline access.</span><span class='zh'>Kage 将任意网站打包成一个独立的可执行文件，离线也能浏览。提高了便携性，适合离线演示、备份与边缘部署。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://roszigit.com/en/blog/timescaledb-compression-hypercore" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How TimescaleDB compresses time-series data</span><span class='zh'>TimescaleDB 如何压缩时序数据</span></a><span class="dom">roszigit.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TimescaleDB compresses time-series data by applying columnar encoding, chunking, and selective compression policies to reduce storage without sacrificing query performance.</span><span class='zh'>TimescaleDB 通过列式编码、分块和有选择的压缩策略来降低时序数据的存储需求，同时尽量保持查询性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TimescaleDB compresses time-series data by applying columnar encoding, chunking, and selective compression policies to reduce storage without sacrificing query performance. The approach helps scale IoT and monitoring workloads, often dramatically lowering costs.</span><span class='zh'>TimescaleDB 通过列式编码、分块和有选择的压缩策略来降低时序数据的存储需求，同时尽量保持查询性能。此做法有助于扩展物联网与监控场景的负载，通常显著降低成本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://karthinks.com/software/even-more-batteries-included-with-emacs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Even more batteries included with Emacs</span><span class='zh'>Emacs 内置功能再升级</span></a><span class="dom">karthinks.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Emacs continues to expand its &#x27;batteries-included&#x27; philosophy, adding more built-in capabilities and sensible defaults that reduce the amount of external configuration needed.</span><span class='zh'>Emacs 进一步扩展了“内置功能即服务”的理念，增加了更多内置能力和更合理的默认设置，减少外部配置的需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Emacs continues to expand its &#x27;batteries-included&#x27; philosophy, adding more built-in capabilities and sensible defaults that reduce the amount of external configuration needed. The piece surveys recent enhancements, from packages to core improvements, and argues the editor remains a capable, self-contained platform for developers and writers.</span><span class='zh'>Emacs 进一步扩展了“内置功能即服务”的理念，增加了更多内置能力和更合理的默认设置，减少外部配置的需求。文章梳理了最近的改进与核心提升，强调该编辑器仍是面向开发者与作者的强大、可自给自足的平台。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-corps" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Corps</span><span class='zh'>Claude Corps（Claude 队）</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic announces Claude Corps, a scalable framework to manage multiple Claude agents with policy and governance controls for enterprises.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Corps，为企业提供可扩展的多代理管理框架，具备策略与治理控制，便于协调 AI 助手并对安全约束进行执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic announces Claude Corps, a scalable framework to manage multiple Claude agents with policy and governance controls for enterprises. The initiative aims to help teams coordinate AI copilots, enforce safety constraints, and scale AI-assisted workflows across organizations.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Corps，为企业提供可扩展的多代理管理框架，具备策略与治理控制，便于协调 AI 助手并对安全约束进行执行。该方案旨在帮助企业在组织内扩展 AI 辅助工作流，同时保障合规性与安全性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stratechery.com/2026/anthropics-safety-superpower/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s Safety Superpower</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的安全护城河</span></a><span class="dom">stratechery.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic bets big on safety as a product differentiator, building guardrails, red-teaming, and policy frameworks into its AI stack.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 将安全性作为产品核心差异化，嵌入模型体系的守护机制、对抗性测试和治理框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic bets big on safety as a product differentiator, building guardrails, red-teaming, and policy frameworks into its AI stack. The argument is that safety-focused design may become a sustainable moat as models scale and policy risk increases; enterprises will pay a premium for reliability and governance.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 将安全性作为产品核心差异化，嵌入模型体系的守护机制、对抗性测试和治理框架。随着模型规模扩大和政策风险上升，安全设计可能成为企业愿意为之付费的护城河，优先考虑可靠性与治理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openrouter.ai/openrouter/fusion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Openrouter Fusion API</span><span class='zh'>Openrouter 融合 API</span></a><span class="dom">openrouter.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Openrouter releases a Fusion API to orchestrate multiple models and tools in a single, cohesive interface, simplifying multi-model prompts and data routing.</span><span class='zh'>Openrouter 发布了融合 API，用于在一个统一接口内编排多种模型和工具，简化多模型提示和数据路由。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Openrouter releases a Fusion API to orchestrate multiple models and tools in a single, cohesive interface, simplifying multi-model prompts and data routing. This lowers integration friction for developers building AI-powered apps that need to combine strengths of different systems.</span><span class='zh'>Openrouter 发布了融合 API，用于在一个统一接口内编排多种模型和工具，简化多模型提示和数据路由。此举降低了将多种系统组合在一起构建 AI 应用的集成难度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Swiss voters rejected a proposal to cap the population at ten million, illustrating limits of regulation aimed at growth control.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士选民否决将人口上限设为一千万的提案，揭示了以增长控制为目标的法规的局限性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Swiss voters rejected a proposal to cap the population at ten million, illustrating limits of regulation aimed at growth control. The decision affects long-term planning, immigration policy, and sustainability debates in a small, wealthy democracy.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士选民否决将人口上限设为一千万的提案，揭示了以增长控制为目标的法规的局限性。此举将影响这座小而富裕民主国家的长期规划、移民政策和可持续性辩论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/ai-and-ml/2026/06/14/ai-is-code-and-cant-be-prompted-into-being-smarter/5254141" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is code – and can&#x27;t be prompted into being smarter</span><span class='zh'>AI 是代码，不能靠提示变得更聪明</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI systems are software, and prompting alone cannot create real intelligence gains; improvements require better models, data, and training.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为 AI 是软件，单靠提示无法真正提升智能水平；改进需改进模型、数据和训练方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI systems are software, and prompting alone cannot create real intelligence gains; improvements require better models, data, and training. It warns against over-reliance on clever prompts and encourages a focus on architecture, safety, and compositional capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为 AI 是软件，单靠提示无法真正提升智能水平；改进需改进模型、数据和训练方式。警示不要过分依赖“聪明的提示”，应关注架构、安全性及系统性能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://economist.com/the-americas/2026/06/11/techno-libertarians-are-flocking-to-the-caribbean" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Techno-libertarians are flocking to the Caribbean</span><span class='zh'>科技自由主义者蜂拥加勒比</span></a><span class="dom">economist.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Techno-libertarians are migrating to Caribbean jurisdictions offering crypto-friendly, low-regulation environments, seeking freedom from perceived surveillance and heavy taxation.</span><span class='zh'>科技自由主义者正涌向加勒比地区的数字友好、低监管环境，寻求摆脱监控与高税负。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Techno-libertarians are migrating to Caribbean jurisdictions offering crypto-friendly, low-regulation environments, seeking freedom from perceived surveillance and heavy taxation.</span><span class='zh'>科技自由主义者正涌向加勒比地区的数字友好、低监管环境，寻求摆脱监控与高税负。文章探讨此趋势对当地经济、治理及科技创业生态的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kobzol.github.io/rust/2026/06/15/how-memory-safety-cves-differ-between-rust-and-c-cpp.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How memory safety CVEs differ between Rust and C/C++</span><span class='zh'>Rust 与 C/C++ 的内存安全 CVE 差异</span></a><span class="dom">kobzol.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Memory-safety CVEs reveal different risk profiles between Rust and C/C++.</span><span class='zh'>内存安全漏洞展示了 Rust 与 C/C++ 在风险类型上的差异。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Memory-safety CVEs reveal different risk profiles between Rust and C/C++. Rust&#x27;s ownership and safer abstractions reduce many classes of vulnerabilities, while C/C++ remain susceptible to use-after-free and unsafe dereferences; the post highlights practical implications for choosing languages in security-sensitive projects.</span><span class='zh'>内存安全漏洞展示了 Rust 与 C/C++ 在风险类型上的差异。Rust 的所有权与更安全的抽象降低了大量漏洞风险，而 C/C++ 仍易出现如使用后释放等问题；这对在安全敏感项目中选用语言具有实际意义。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A government report suggests federal data-center rules are being allowed to expire, potentially before replacements or updates are in place.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，美国政府允许联邦数据中心相关规则到期，可能在未有替代或更新时出现空缺。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A government report suggests federal data-center rules are being allowed to expire, potentially before replacements or updates are in place. The move could affect energy efficiency, security standards, and cloud migration plans across agencies. Critics warn about loss of modernization momentum and resilience.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，美国政府允许联邦数据中心相关规则到期，可能在未有替代或更新时出现空缺。此举可能影响各机构的能效、安全标准与云迁移计划。批评者担忧将减缓现代化与韧性提升的步伐。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nick.zoic.org/art/boot-naked-linux/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Boot Naked Linux</span><span class='zh'>裸机启动 Linux</span></a><span class="dom">nick.zoic.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A minimalist approach to booting Linux, stripping away optional initramfs or init-time dependencies to showcase a lean, fast startup path.</span><span class='zh'>以极简方式启动 Linux，去除可选的 initramfs 与初始化依赖，展示一种更精简、启动更快的路径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A minimalist approach to booting Linux, stripping away optional initramfs or init-time dependencies to showcase a lean, fast startup path. The project emphasizes educational value and potential security benefits of a naked-boot approach.</span><span class='zh'>以极简方式启动 Linux，去除可选的 initramfs 与初始化依赖，展示一种更精简、启动更快的路径。该项目强调此类裸机启动在教育与潜在安全方面的价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rio de Janeiro&#x27;s &quot;homegrown&quot; LLM appears to be a merge of an existing model</span><span class='zh'>里约热内卢的‘本土化’大模型似乎是现有模型的合并</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Early analysis suggests the Rio de Janeiro project reuses components from an existing LLM rather than training an original one from scratch.</span><span class='zh'>初步分析表明，该项目在现有大模型基础上混合组件，而非完全从头训练。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Early analysis suggests the Rio de Janeiro project reuses components from an existing LLM rather than training an original one from scratch. This provenance has implications for licensing, attribution, and how the model behaves in practice.</span><span class='zh'>初步分析表明，该项目在现有大模型基础上混合组件，而非完全从头训练。这对许可、署名和实际表现等方面都可能产生影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ponytail – make your AI agent think like the laziest senior dev in the room</span><span class='zh'>Ponytail：让 AI 助手像最懒的资深开发者一样思考</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ponytail is a tool to steer AI agents toward the pragmatic, low-effort reasoning style of a senior developer, emphasizing efficient prompts and sensible defaults.</span><span class='zh'>Ponytail 是一个工具，帮助 AI 代理模仿资深开发者的务实、低成本思维方式，强调高效提示与合理默认。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ponytail is a tool to steer AI agents toward the pragmatic, low-effort reasoning style of a senior developer, emphasizing efficient prompts and sensible defaults. It aims to improve reliability and speed of AI-assisted coding.</span><span class='zh'>Ponytail 是一个工具，帮助 AI 代理模仿资深开发者的务实、低成本思维方式，强调高效提示与合理默认。目标是提升 AI 辅助编码的可靠性与速度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://wizardzines.com/comics/write-for-one-person/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Write for One Person</span><span class='zh'>为一个人写作</span></a><span class="dom">wizardzines.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A zine-style comic argues writing for one reader can sharpen voice, clarity, and empathy by tailoring language, tone, and structure to a single audience.</span><span class='zh'>一本漫画风格的专刊主张为单一读者而写能磨炼语气、表达清晰度与共情能力，强调用一个目标读者来定调语言、语气与结构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A zine-style comic argues writing for one reader can sharpen voice, clarity, and empathy by tailoring language, tone, and structure to a single audience. It offers practical examples and prompts to practice concise, intimate prose.</span><span class='zh'>一本漫画风格的专刊主张为单一读者而写能磨炼语气、表达清晰度与共情能力，强调用一个目标读者来定调语言、语气与结构。提供可操作的练习与示例，帮助练习简洁而贴近的写作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.co.uk/programmes/w3ct8k70" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why does paper fold so well?</span><span class='zh'>纸为何能折叠得这么好？</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An explainer delves into the physics of paper folding, showing how thickness, crease propagation, and layering govern how reliably a crease holds and how many folds you can make.</span><span class='zh'>一则科普解说纸张折叠的物理原理，揭示厚度、皱褶传播与层叠等因素如何影响折痕的稳定性与可折叠次数。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An explainer delves into the physics of paper folding, showing how thickness, crease propagation, and layering govern how reliably a crease holds and how many folds you can make. The takeaway for makers is that geometry and material properties, not just technique, determine how a sheet behaves when folded.</span><span class='zh'>一则科普解说纸张折叠的物理原理，揭示厚度、皱褶传播与层叠等因素如何影响折痕的稳定性与可折叠次数。对创作者而言，关键在于几何结构与材料特性共同决定折叠时的表现。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how Lisp-inspired ideas shaped Ruby&#x27;s flexibility, metaprogramming culture, and a penchant for DSLs, showing that Lisp’s legacy lives on in modern dynamic languages.</span><span class='zh'>本文解析 Lisp 传统中的理念如何影响 Ruby 的灵活性、元编程文化以及对领域特定语言的偏好，展示 Lisp 的遗产在现代动态语言中的延续。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how Lisp-inspired ideas shaped Ruby&#x27;s flexibility, metaprogramming culture, and a penchant for DSLs, showing that Lisp’s legacy lives on in modern dynamic languages.</span><span class='zh'>本文解析 Lisp 传统中的理念如何影响 Ruby 的灵活性、元编程文化以及对领域特定语言的偏好，展示 Lisp 的遗产在现代动态语言中的延续。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://planetscale.com/blog/the-only-scalable-delete" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The only scalable delete in Postgres is DROP TABLE</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 中唯一可扩展的删除操作是 DROP TABLE</span></a><span class="dom">planetscale.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Argues that deleting vast amounts of data in Postgres scales best with dropping tables rather than row-level DELETEs; discusses implications for archival strategies and schema design.</span><span class='zh'>指出在 Postgres 中，海量数据的删除最具可扩展性的做法是 DROP TABLE，而非逐行 DELETE；并探讨归档与模式设计的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Argues that deleting vast amounts of data in Postgres scales best with dropping tables rather than row-level DELETEs; discusses implications for archival strategies and schema design.</span><span class='zh'>指出在 Postgres 中，海量数据的删除最具可扩展性的做法是 DROP TABLE，而非逐行 DELETE；并探讨归档与模式设计的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gamma correction is essential for accurate color rendering in images and displays, yet it&#x27;s often misunderstood in software.</span><span class='zh'>伽玛校正是图像和显示器色彩正确呈现的关键，但在工程实践中常被误解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gamma correction is essential for accurate color rendering in images and displays, yet it&#x27;s often misunderstood in software. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A recent post demonstrates factoring RSA keys with polynomial techniques, highlighting vulnerabilities when keys are insufficiently sized or poorly generated.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究演示了利用多项式方法分解 RSA 密钥，暴露在密钥尺寸不足或参数生成不当时的脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A recent post demonstrates factoring RSA keys with polynomial techniques, highlighting vulnerabilities when keys are insufficiently sized or poorly generated. The result underscores the importance of appropriate key sizes and safe parameter choices to prevent practical attacks. It serves as a reminder that cryptography remains highly dependent on careful implementation.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究演示了利用多项式方法分解 RSA 密钥，暴露在密钥尺寸不足或参数生成不当时的脆弱性。此结果强调了选取合适密钥长度与安全参数的重要性，以及对现代密码学安全实践的持续警示。提醒开发者在实现中保持谨慎。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tokyopaladin.substack.com/p/foreign-business-owners-are-scrambling" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Foreign business owners are scrambling to raise capital to stay in Japan</span><span class='zh'>外国企业主正在拼命筹资以留在日本</span></a><span class="dom">tokyopaladin.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Foreign business owners in Japan are scrambling to raise capital to stay in the country, signaling tightening funding conditions and regulatory or visa pressures for non-Japanese operators.</span><span class='zh'>日本的外国企业主正在拼命筹集资金以留在日本，这反映出融资环境收紧以及对非日籍经营者的签证与监管压力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Foreign business owners in Japan are scrambling to raise capital to stay in the country, signaling tightening funding conditions and regulatory or visa pressures for non-Japanese operators.</span><span class='zh'>日本的外国企业主正在拼命筹集资金以留在日本，这反映出融资环境收紧以及对非日籍经营者的签证与监管压力。该文章凸显了外国资本企业的困境及其对日本创业生态的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/YusufB5/ASCILINE" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Asciline – real-time ASCII video rendering engine</span><span class='zh'>Asciline：实时 ASCII 视频渲染引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asciline is a real-time ASCII video renderer that converts video frames into ASCII art on the fly, enabling lightweight, terminal-friendly previews or retro aesthetics.</span><span class='zh'>Asciline 是一个实时的 ASCII 视频渲染引擎，能够在传输过程中将视频帧转换成 ASCII 艺术，适合轻量级终端预览和复古风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asciline is a real-time ASCII video renderer that converts video frames into ASCII art on the fly, enabling lightweight, terminal-friendly previews or retro aesthetics. The project demonstrates efficient streaming and clever text-based rendering techniques that can run with minimal dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>Asciline 是一个实时的 ASCII 视频渲染引擎，能够在传输过程中将视频帧转换成 ASCII 艺术，适合轻量级终端预览和复古风格。该项目展示了高效流式处理和文本渲染技术，依赖极少。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A concise history of Cerro Torre, the Patagonian peak famed for its dramatic climbs and controversial ascent disputed by climbers and guidebooks, tracing expeditions, contested routes, and the ethics of style in extreme mountaineering.</span><span class='zh'>简要回顾巴塔哥尼亚 Cerro Torre 的历史——因极端攀登、被质疑的攀登记录以及对“风格与伦理”的持续辩论而著称。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A concise history of Cerro Torre, the Patagonian peak famed for its dramatic climbs and controversial ascent disputed by climbers and guidebooks, tracing expeditions, contested routes, and the ethics of style in extreme mountaineering.</span><span class='zh'>简要回顾巴塔哥尼亚 Cerro Torre 的历史——因极端攀登、被质疑的攀登记录以及对“风格与伦理”的持续辩论而著称。文章梳理了多次探险、被争议的路线，以及极限攀登中的风格问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/meta-pytorch/torchcodec/releases/tag/v0.14.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TorchCodec 0.14: HDR Video Decoding for CPU and CUDA, and Fast Wav Decoder</span><span class='zh'>TorchCodec 0.14：CPU 与 CUDA 的 HDR 视频解码，以及快速 WAV 解码器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TorchCodec 0.14 adds HDR video decoding support on CPU and CUDA, expanding hardware-accelerated media processing for PyTorch workflows, plus a fast WAV decoder to speed audio paths.</span><span class='zh'>TorchCodec 0.14 增加了对 CPU 与 CUDA 的 HDR 视频解码支持，提升 PyTorch 工作流中的多媒体处理能力，并提供一个快速 WAV 解码器以加速音频路径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TorchCodec 0.14 adds HDR video decoding support on CPU and CUDA, expanding hardware-accelerated media processing for PyTorch workflows, plus a fast WAV decoder to speed audio paths. The update targets ML and media apps needing efficient, high-quality video and audio handling.</span><span class='zh'>TorchCodec 0.14 增加了对 CPU 与 CUDA 的 HDR 视频解码支持，提升 PyTorch 工作流中的多媒体处理能力，并提供一个快速 WAV 解码器以加速音频路径。此次更新面向需要高效、质量并重的视频与音频处理的机器学习与多媒体应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://0xkrt26.github.io/math_behind_security/2026/06/09/the-story-of-the-hash-function.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chopped, Stored, Secured – The Story of the Hash Function</span><span class='zh'>哈希函数的故事：切碎、存储、安全</span></a><span class="dom">0xkrt26.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Charts the history of hash functions from early constructions to modern cryptographic primitives, explaining how they enable data integrity checks, quick lookups, and secure authentication.</span><span class='zh'>梳理哈希函数的历史，从早期构造到现代密码学原语，解释哈希在数据完整性、快速查找与身份认证中的作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Charts the history of hash functions from early constructions to modern cryptographic primitives, explaining how they enable data integrity checks, quick lookups, and secure authentication. The piece traces the lifecycle from input processing to storage and comparison, highlighting security pitfalls and best practices.</span><span class='zh'>梳理哈希函数的历史，从早期构造到现代密码学原语，解释哈希在数据完整性、快速查找与身份认证中的作用。文章回顾从输入处理、存储到比对的全过程，强调安全要点与最佳实践。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fulcrum.inc/2026/06/09/inverse-rubric-optimization.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inverse Rubric Optimization: A testbed for agent science</span><span class='zh'>逆向评量标准优化：代理科学的测试床</span></a><span class="dom">fulcrum.inc</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Inverse Rubric Optimization presents a framework to frame agent behavior in terms of evaluative rubrics that can be inverted to study alignment, incentive design, and evaluation dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>逆向评量标准优化提供了一种将代理行为以评估性准则来框定的框架，并可对准则进行反演研究对齐、激励设计和评估动力学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Inverse Rubric Optimization presents a framework to frame agent behavior in terms of evaluative rubrics that can be inverted to study alignment, incentive design, and evaluation dynamics. It offers a testbed for experimenting with how goals translate into observable actions.</span><span class='zh'>逆向评量标准优化提供了一种将代理行为以评估性准则来框定的框架，并可对准则进行反演研究对齐、激励设计和评估动力学。它为研究目标如何转化为可观察行动提供了测试床。</span></p>
